## Final cross-cutting review: network resilience (a42c385..dcec090)

**Verdict: With fixes.** The design is sound and the money paths are safe against replays. Before merge, fix the device-clock refusal (C1) and the four outbox and deploy issues (I1–I4). Each is small. I read the code at HEAD directly, file by file, rather than relying on earlier task reviews. I did not run the test suites.

### Strengths
- **Replays are safe in depth.** Purchases have a key saved with the item, a replay check in `record_purchase` that runs before the time check (R11), a second check under the ledger lock, and a unique `ledger_entries.idempotency_key`. Two tabs, or a missing Web Lock, can only cause replays, never a second debit. The scripted check confirmed one POST, one debit and one history row.
- **Wrong-child protection holds.** The body `userId` must equal the session user, or the route returns 401 and the item stays waiting (R14). The purchase key is scoped to the child, and `childId` always comes from the session, never the payload.
- **Timeouts never sign anyone out.** The synthetic 503 JA503 (R4) is handled the same way everywhere:
  - The proxy returns early when the session is unknown.
  - `getCurrentUser` throws UNAVAILABLE instead of returning null.
  - ProfileGate and ChildOutboxSync fail open.
- **Session and admin rules hold.** There is no `getSession` on the server. The guide only uses the admin client after `findGuideAccess`, and replay runs after that switch (R9).
- **Grants are right.** `resolve_occurred_at`, `record_purchase_at`, `guide_record_turn` and `guide_find_turn` are revoked from `authenticated`. No new tables, so RLS is unchanged.
- **The outbox is well built.** `nextState` is pure and covered by tests. Order is kept by letting the oldest waiting item hold the queue. Each user's items are isolated through the `userId` index and per-user snapshots.
- **No barrel cycles.** offline never imports diary (R2/R17). The `offlineCopy` strings are used consistently.

### Issues

#### Critical (Must Fix)

**C1. Any device whose clock is more than 60 s fast is refused on every purchase, practice answer and mission completion, with copy that blames the child.**
- **Where:** `src/features/offline/outbox/submit.ts:36` (`occurredAt: input.occurredAt ?? now`, device clock), `src/features/diary/components/purchase-sheet.tsx:89` (`occurredAt: new Date()…`), `supabase/migrations/20261030130002_device_occurred_at.sql:22`.
- **Why it breaks:** every outbox item, online sends included, now carries the device clock. `resolve_occurred_at` raises 22023 when that time is more than 60 s ahead, and `db-error` maps 22023 to "Please check what you entered and try again."
- **Effects:**
  - Purchases are refused at submit, every time, on that device. Before this change, online purchases used server `now()` and worked.
  - Practice and completeMission are refused with a red message. `stepDone` returns false, so the child is stuck on the step.
  - Queued learning results that are refused later are deleted silently (R18).
- **Why tests miss it:** test machines have synced clocks. Laptops and kids' tablets that drift, or have a hand-set clock, are not rare.
- **Fix (preferred):** send the age of the action, measured on the device's own clock (`ageMs = Date.now() - Date.parse(occurredAt)`), and let the server compute `now() - least(age, 7 days)`. The clock offset cancels out.
- **Fix (minimum):** clamp a future time to `now()` instead of raising, and send `occurredAt` only for items that actually waited.
- Add route tests for the practice and completeMission branches at the same time (this closes the Task 7 deferred item).

#### Important (Should Fix)

**I1. While offline with the app open, a purchase "waits too long" after about 17 minutes, not 7 days.**
- **Where:** `src/features/offline/components/outbox-sync.tsx:33-37` (30 s poll), `outbox/flush.ts` `drain`, `outbox/state.ts:33`.
- **What happens:** nothing checks `navigator.onLine` before sending. Each offline poll does a failed fetch, which counts as a retry and adds an attempt. With backoff of 2/5/15/30/60 s, attempt 20 arrives in roughly 17 minutes. The purchase becomes "This one waited too long. Add it again?" The learning items are deleted (R18b).
- **Fix:** skip the drain while `navigator.onLine === false`, and/or count only attempts that failed while the device thought it was online.

**I2. "Change it" on an expired purchase can debit Spend twice (a spec anti-goal).**
- **Where:** `src/features/diary/components/pending-purchases.tsx:80-86` together with `state.ts:33-40`.
- **What happens:** an item expires after a `retry` outcome, which includes a 15 s client timeout or a 503 where the server may already have committed. Change it mints a new key and removes the old item. If the last attempt did commit, Spend is debited twice. It is unlikely (the last attempt must have committed with its response lost), but the copy ("Add it again?") invites exactly this.
- **Fix:** before opening the sheet for an expired item, re-send the old item once. Replay is checked before the time check, so the result is reliable:
  - `done` means it was already added: drop the item and refresh.
  - A refusal proves it was never recorded: open the sheet.

**I3. The new migrations break "older code keeps working against the newer schema" (stated in `docs/deploy.md:71`).**
- **Where:** `20261030130000_retry_safe_gift.sql:21` drops `create_gift(uuid,bigint,date,text)` and adds a required 5th parameter. `20261030130001_retry_safe_guide_turn.sql:23` drops the 10-argument `guide_record_turn` and adds a required `p_client_message_id`.
- **Effects:**
  - Supabase applies migrations in parallel with the Vercel build. During that window, gift creation fails, and every guide turn fails after Claude has already been paid for.
  - A Vercel "Promote previous deployment" rollback leaves both broken.
  - `record_purchase`, `complete_mission` and `record_practice_result` are fine because their new parameter has a default.
- **Fix:** `p_idempotency_key text default null` (null means `gen_random_uuid()`), and `p_client_message_id uuid default null` (the unique index is already partial).

**I4. The outbox needs IndexedDB, so a child without it can never record a purchase or save a learning step.**
- **Where:** `outbox/submit.ts:27-28` returns `unsaved` when `open()` is null (private mode, blocked storage, a broken IDB). The online `recordPurchaseAction` was removed.
- **What the child sees:** "You're offline, try again when connected", which is wrong because they are online.
- **Fix:** when there is no store and the device is online, call `sendToServer` directly with an in-memory item (same key) and map the outcome. Show the offline copy only when actually offline.

**I5. "Try again" on a slow page reloads into the browser's offline error page.**
- **Where:** `src/components/shell/slow-fallback.tsx:32-35`.
- **What happens:** offline, a client navigation shows the skeleton, the slow copy appears at 10 s, the child taps Try again, and 5 s later `window.location.reload()` runs. The open app (the precondition for offline saving) is replaced by the browser's offline page.
- **Fix:** skip the reload, and show `offlineCopy.offline`, when `navigator.onLine === false` or `useOnline()` is false.

**I6. ProfileGate swallows Next's prerender errors (the controller's item; confirmed).**
- **Where:** `src/app/(app)/profile-gate.tsx:22`.
- **Cause:** `getCurrentUser` awaits `connection()`. During a prerender, that promise rejects with `HangingPromiseRejectionError` once the prerender completes, and the widened catch logs it as a failure.
- **Does it change rendering?** I believe not. The rejection only arrives after the prerender has finished, so the boundary is already a dynamic hole and the returned `null` is thrown away. At request time `connection()` resolves normally, and the dialog still shows (the e2e `parent-profile` test passes).
- **Real cost:** false warns, 52 per build and 12 at `pnpm start` (likely runtime/prefetch prerenders). That noise buries the real "profile lookup failed" signal the fail-open design relies on.
- **Fix:** call `unstable_rethrow(error)` first in the catch, as `child/layout.tsx` ChildOutboxSync already does. It covers hanging-promise and prerender-interrupted errors, and Next router errors. Add a test that a `HangingPromiseRejection`-shaped error is rethrown.

**I7. The spec's "treat already in that state as success" was not done, and the new 15 s timeout now invites the retry that triggers it.**
- **Where:** `allowance-summary.tsx` (resume/stop), `pause-sheet.tsx`, `gift-form.tsx` CancelGiftForm, create allowance.
- **What happens:** after "This is taking a while. Try again?", the parent's retry of a step that actually succeeded gets JA409 ("Allowance is not paused", "Gift is no longer pending") as a red error.
- **Fix:** map these CONFLICT replies to success in the client wrapper for those calls (stop → stopped, resume → active, cancel → cancelled), then refresh.

#### Minor (Nice to Have)

- **M1.** `src/lib/timed-fetch.ts:73`: the timer is cleared once headers arrive, so a body that stalls after headers has no deadline (the spec's "stuck socket"). Buffer the body inside the deadline (`await response.arrayBuffer()` and return a new `Response`); Supabase bodies are small.
- **M2.** `use-online.ts:28` and `online-action.ts:62`: a single failed call sets the banner to "You're offline", and it stays until some later call succeeds (R12). Page navigations never clear it. Also, any thrown server-action error is labelled offline. Clear it on the next successful navigation or fetch, and only report a failure for `TypeError` fetch failures.
- **M3.** `gift-form.tsx:52`: `useState(() => crypto.randomUUID())` runs during SSR and again on the client, which gives a hydration attribute mismatch on the hidden input. It also throws in insecure contexts. Use the `useEffect` pattern from handover-form, or `newId`. `guide-sheet.tsx:116` should also use `newId`.
- **M4.** `auth/server/actions.ts:57`: if `_useSession` fails (expired access token while Supabase is down), auth-js returns `sessionError` without removing the session. The redirect to /login then bounces back to the dashboard (the user is still signed in), so sign-out silently does nothing. Spec: "Sign-out works or says it didn't." Return UNAVAILABLE in that case.
- **M5.** `submit.ts:55`: when the drain is busy (another flush in this tab or another tab), an online submit returns `queued`. The child sees the "Waiting to send… when you're back online" toast while online, for example on two quick purchases. Await the `again` pass, or use neutral copy.
- **M6.** Calm styling is not consistent across three components. FormError's calm style is plain muted text; purchase-sheet's `unsaved` and "Ask a parent" use a `bg-muted` box; the mission player has its own `Problem` style. The purchase refusal is still red (Task 8 deferred item). Pick one calm style.
- **M7.** 22023 "Check the time on this device" reaches the child as "Please check what you entered and try again." Map the time error to its own child-safe sentence (largely moot once C1 is fixed).
- **M8.** Check-in and step do not carry `occurredAt` (the schemas strip it), but the spec lists check-in "today". An offline check-in at 23:50 counts the next day. Document the gap or add it.
- **M9.** `src/app/error.tsx`: every error now says "Check your connection", including real bugs. Acceptable, since production strips the error code, but note it in `docs/design.md`.
- **M10.** `src/lib/supabase/{server,proxy,admin}.ts` import `@/lib/timed-fetch`. That is a deep alias inside the same module; the rule says relative.
- **M11.** `src/app/api/v1/outbox/schema.ts` imports the `@/features/diary` client barrel, which now pulls in `pending-purchases`, then `@/features/offline`, then `idb`, into the route bundle. The Task 7 concern survives R17 by another path. Export `purchaseSchema` from a schema-only entry, or accept it.
- **M12.** `proxy.ts`: if a token refresh reached Supabase (and rotated the token) but the 8 s timeout fired, the browser keeps the old refresh token. That is fine within the 30 s reuse interval; after that the user is signed out. It is an edge case, but worth noting in `docs/auth.md`.
- **M13. The 8 components that set state after an await outside a transition:** overspend-switch, undo-button, allowance-summary (`useIdAction`), handover-form (NotGivenButton), gift-form (CancelGiftForm), mission-player (`begin`/`finish`), guide-switch, guide-sheet.
  - **Effect:** a one-render window where the new state or error shows while `pending` still disables the control. A tap in that frame is lost, as rule-row showed.
  - **Not affected:** money and data. Retries are keyed, and guide-sheet has a generation guard.
  - **Severity:** low. The two switches (overspend-switch, guide-switch) are the most noticeable.
  - **Fix:** the same `start(() => …)` wrap as commit 6084134. Do it as one follow-up commit.

### Declined to judge
- No Service Worker / cold start offline: an explicit non-goal.
- Needs-look purchases never expiring, and other users' parked items staying on a shared device: matches the spec ("stays until the child changes or removes it").
- Repository-level timing: done once in timed-fetch instead of per repository, which meets the spec's intent.
- Skipping refresh on prefetch requests in the proxy: the spec leaves it to the builder, and the 30 s reuse interval plus timeouts ship.
- Copy wording choices beyond child safety (e.g. "Not sent yet" heading): a design call.
- `maxDuration` 60 vs the Hobby/Fluid 300 s default: an intended cap (R6/R2 note).
- Removed or PIN-reset child JWT staying valid up to an hour: the spec says keep as is.
- The route mapping not_enough_spend to a 409 business outcome: sanctioned by R2.
- Backdated purchases avoiding spend-limit notices: accepted trade-off in `domain.md`.
- The Playwright/CDP 3G results: I took the Task 13 report as written and did not re-run it.

### Recommendations
1. One short fix wave, one commit per item: C1 (age-based time plus route tests), I1, I3, I6, I4, I5, I2 (plus a Change it e2e), then I7.
2. Re-run `pnpm db:reset && pnpm db:test`. Add pgTAP for the defaulted `create_gift`/`guide_record_turn` calls and for clamping a fast clock.
3. Batch M13 and M6 as a follow-up cleanup.

### Assessment
**Ready to merge?** With fixes
**Reasoning:** Replay protection, wrong-child protection and session handling are solid. But C1 breaks the core purchase and learning paths on any device whose clock runs fast, and I1–I4 undermine the offline promise and safe rollback. Each is a small, local change.

## Fix list (do all; one focused commit per item, Conventional Commits, ≤60-char subject, body lines ≤100, NO AI attribution, include .claude-logs)
1. **C1 / R26 — device clock.** Outbox sends the action's age, not wall-clock time.
   - `src/features/offline/outbox/send.ts`: payload carries `ageMs = Math.max(0, Date.now() - Date.parse(item.createdAt))`; stop sending `occurredAt`.
   - Callers (purchase-sheet `occurredAt: new Date()…`, submit.ts `occurredAt ?? now`) no longer need a client time. The item's own `createdAt` is enough; keep `occurredAt` on the item only if expiry needs it.
   - `src/app/api/v1/outbox/route.ts` + `schema.ts`: accept `ageMs` (int ≥ 0), strip any client `occurredAt`, compute `occurredAt = new Date(Date.now() - Math.min(ageMs, 7 days)).toISOString()` on the server, and pass it to the services.
   - SQL `resolve_occurred_at` (migration 20261030130002_device_occurred_at.sql — unpushed, edit in place): a future time clamps to now() instead of raising; the 7-days-back check stays as a backstop.
   - Map the 22023 time refusal to child-safe copy if it can still reach a child.
   - Add the missing route tests for practice, step and completeMission (including that the server-computed occurredAt reaches completeMission/recordPractice).
   - pgTAP: a fast clock clamps to now(); an 8-day-old time still raises.
   - Unit test: a device clock 1 h fast still records with ageMs≈0.
2. **I1 / R27.** `flush.ts` drain does nothing (no attempt counted) while `navigator.onLine === false`. Test.
3. **I3 / R29.** Edit the migrations in place (unpushed):
   - `create_gift(..., p_idempotency_key text default null)`: null means gen_random_uuid().
   - `guide_record_turn(..., p_client_message_id uuid default null)`.
   - `pnpm db:reset && pnpm db:types` (commit types if changed). pgTAP: both callable with the old arity.
4. **I6 / R32.** `src/app/(app)/profile-gate.tsx`: `unstable_rethrow(error)` first in the catch. Test that a Next control-flow / prerender-style error is rethrown (mirror ChildOutboxSync's approach).
5. **I4 / R30.** `submit.ts`: when the store can't open and `navigator.onLine !== false`, send directly via sendToServer with an in-memory item (same key) and map the outcome to sent / refused / queued-not-possible. Return `unsaved` only when actually offline. Test.
6. **I5 / R31.** `src/components/shell/slow-fallback.tsx`: never call `window.location.reload()` while offline (`navigator.onLine === false` or `useOnline()` false). Show `offlineCopy.offline` instead. Tests.
7. **I2 / R28.** `pending-purchases.tsx` "Change it" on an **expired** item first re-sends the old item once:
   - done → remove it, refresh, toast "Saved. Nice one.";
   - refused → open the sheet prefilled with a NEW key;
   - retry/unauthorized/offline → keep the item, show the offline copy.
   - Add a Playwright e2e covering Change it (closes the Task 8 deferred item).
8. **I7 / R33.** For resume/stop/pause allowance (allowance-summary, pause-sheet) and cancel gift (gift-form CancelGiftForm): a CONFLICT reply where the target state is already reached counts as success (refresh + the normal success path). Keep genuine conflicts as errors. Decide "already reached" from the specific JA409 message/code the SQL raises — read the migrations. Tests.
9. **R34 small items:**
   - **M1** `src/lib/timed-fetch.ts`: read the body inside the deadline (buffer it and return a new Response with the same status/headers), so a stalled body also times out. Test.
   - **M2** `use-online.ts` / `online-action.ts`: `reportNetworkFailure` only for real network failures (a rejected fetch TypeError / Next's "Failed to fetch"), not any thrown error. Clear the reported failure on a successful navigation. If that's hard, on a successful same-origin fetch from the outbox / runOnline is enough — say which. Tests.
   - **M3** gift-form and guide-sheet keys: no SSR/client hydration mismatch (handover-form's useEffect pattern or mint on first submit) and use `newId`. Test.
   - **M4** `src/features/auth/server/actions.ts` signOut: if auth-js returns an error AND the session was not removed (e.g. `_useSession` failure — read GoTrueClient `_signOut`), return `AppError("UNAVAILABLE", …)` instead of redirecting into a bounce. Otherwise keep the redirect. Test.
   - **M10** `src/lib/supabase/{server,proxy,admin}.ts` import timed-fetch relatively.
   - **M13** wrap post-await state updates in the transition (as commit 6084134 did in rule-row.tsx) in overspend-switch, undo-button, allowance-summary (useIdAction), handover-form (NotGivenButton), gift-form (CancelGiftForm), mission-player (begin/finish), guide-switch, guide-sheet. One commit.
